- **Operator-confirmed locks from session 40 (do not re-debate without flag):**
  - **WelcomeModal "fresh visit" semantics.** Shows on every fresh page-load of `/` (F5, new tab, direct URL). In-app navigation between routes does NOT re-trigger. localStorage and sessionStorage deliberately not used. State lives in React only — captured once via `useState(() => location.pathname === "/")` lazy initializer in `App.tsx`. Subsequent re-renders don't re-evaluate the initial state.
  - **Combined visual approach for the modal:** lucide icons + inline SVGs. Lucide icon as a small section-label marker (~18px, purple accent, inline with heading); inline SVG diagram as the visual content for that section. Operator explicitly approved despite the "simple modal" framing — judged the layered hierarchy worth the slight scope expansion.
  - **Reddit throttle default = 1.5s/req.** Smoke test at this throttle returned 50/50 ok; live full-corpus run returned 5028/5061 ok with no 429s. Don't lower without re-validation; the rate-limit ceiling is unknown.
  - **Conservative tombstone rule held at scale.** 0 tombstones on the full 5061-corpus run is the right answer for a healthy corpus, not a sign the rule is too strict. 33 × http_403s deliberately left alive (retailer bot-blocks; same TLS-fingerprint problem `catalog_discovery` solves).
  - **9 narrative-heavy wave-item lines in TASKS.md stay as-is.** Operator picked "leave them" over "extend the trim" or "loosen CLAUDE.md rule" — they carry load-bearing pilot state and don't violate the session-N / sub-bite parts of the flat-checklist rule.
- **Live findings from session 40 (operator visibility):**
  - **The throttle worked completely at scale.** Smoke 50/50 ok → live 5028/5061 ok. Zero http_429. The session-38 90.6% 429 rate was entirely attributable to anonymous concurrent HEAD requests at workers=16; serializing reddit-host requests behind a single `threading.Lock` with a 1.5s sleep gate fully resolves it.
  - **No genuine link rot in the corpus.** 0 tombstones means every reddit / news / video / article URL still responded ok or with a 403 retailer-block (which is a transient block, not a delete). The corpus is intact at this snapshot. Future quarterly refreshes will re-verify automatically through `scripts/refresh.py`.
  - **Wall-clock matched the prediction.** 2h 07m for 5061 URLs at 1.5s reddit throttle is within minutes of the wrap's "~2hr 09m" extrapolation. The 163 non-reddit URLs fly through at ~0.2s each; the 4898 reddit URLs dominate the wall-clock at 1.5s each.
  - **Frontend test framework was due.** Session-39 wrap explicitly flagged "Frontend has no test framework" — Vitest + RTL + jest-dom landed cleanly with 13 first-pass tests covering both pure functions (`briefToMarkdown`) and components (`WelcomeModal`). Test runs in ~1.3s; npm scripts in place. Future bites can drop tests next to source without setup overhead.
  - **`tsc` caught a non-trivial config issue.** First-pass `vite.config.ts` used `defineConfig` from `vite` with a `/// <reference types="vitest" />` triple-slash directive. tsc -b under tsconfig.node.json compiled the config but still failed: "Object literal may only specify known properties, and 'test' does not exist in type 'UserConfigExport'." Switching to `import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config"` (which re-exports vite's defineConfig with merged types) fixed it cleanly. Worth knowing for any future vite plugin that extends `UserConfigExport`.
  - **Cumulative LLM spend across sessions: ~$20.40** (unchanged from sessions 36–39; session 40 made zero LLM calls — verifier is HTTP HEAD only, frontend work is local, packet draft is read-from-DB only).

- **Code structure shipped (session 39):**
  - **Bite #6 — Quarterly-cadence scheduler skeleton.** New `pulse_check/scheduling/__init__.py` + `pulse_check/scheduling/state.py` (~110 LOC). `RefreshState` frozen dataclass with `(last_refresh_at, last_refresh_run_id)` + `RefreshState.never()` factory. `read_state(path)` returns `never()` on missing file; raises `ValueError` on schema-version mismatch (no silent forward-version coercion). `write_state(state, path)` is atomic: write to a sibling temp file under the same directory, then `os.replace()` into place — cannot leave a half-written file. `is_due(state, *, now, interval_days=90)` returns True for never-refreshed OR gap >= interval. `days_since_refresh(state, *, now)` returns whole-day count or None. New `scripts/refresh.py` (~140 LOC) — orchestrator. Dry-run by default; `--execute` opt-in + y/N keypress confirm; `--force` bypasses is_due (still asks). Subprocess chain `scrape.py → run_stage_b.py → verify_mention_links.py` invoked via `sys.executable` with `cwd=PROJECT_ROOT`. State is written ONLY after every step exits 0 (per `feedback_no_auto_rerun_on_crash`). - **Operator-confirmed locks from session 39 (do not re-debate without flag):**
  - **`refresh.py` dry-run is the default.** Bare invocation prints the plan + cost estimate + due-verdict without firing any subprocess. `--execute` is the opt-in. `--force` bypasses the 90-day gate. y/N keypress confirmation runs on every `--execute`, even with `--force`. Rationale: a scheduler-misfire that auto-fires would burn $5-25 of LLM credit before anyone noticed.
  - **The Windows Task Scheduler XML fires `refresh_quarterly.ps1` which invokes `refresh.py` WITHOUT `--execute`.** Scheduled task only produces a dry-run plan in `data/refresh_log/`; operator manually runs `--execute` after reviewing. This is the "dry-run only, alert me" fork the operator picked. The XML template lists `PLACEHOLDER_REPO_ROOT` + `PLACEHOLDER_DOMAIN\PLACEHOLDER_USER` for the operator to fill before import.
  - **Refresh state lives in `data/refresh_state.json`**, NOT in the DB (no `Run.last_refresh_at` column added). Single-row JSON with `schema_version: 1` for future migration. Implementation: new `pulse_check/verifiers/reddit_verifier.py` adjacent to the existing httpx-based one; routes by URL host. Cost: $0 LLM but adds a deps line + secrets to manage. Risk: reddit auth flow can be brittle; need a registered app.

Skips mentions where `tombstoned_at IS NOT NULL`.

Silences `httpx` INFO log chatter (5k+ requests would flood the console). Opens a single `httpx.Client()` for the run.

The drop is necessary because `_compute_bucket` already tolerates missing mentions in a way that would still increment polarity / intensity / total_mentions counts for a tombstoned mention — fix prevents arithmetic drift while keeping the warning path intact for genuinely orphaned tags. Re-checks the "empty-on-both-sides" early-out after the filter so we don't emit zero-mention aggregate rows post-tombstone.

- **Operator-confirmed locks from session 38 (do not re-debate without flag):**
  - **Tombstone rule is conservative**: only `http_404` / `http_410` / `dns_fail` / `conn_refused` set `tombstoned_at`. 403 / 5xx / timeout / generic-transport-error leave the mention alive. Rationale: a single bad day for an upstream host (or a retailer bot-block) shouldn't permanently drop real citations from briefs.
  - **Aggregation filter is forward-only.** New runs filter tombstoned mentions out of mention-pool fetch + drop their tags; existing `run_wave5_v1` aggregates are frozen by their baked-in `mention_ids` arrays. This contradicts the prior ARCH §9 "remain contributors" stance — operator approved during the design proposal.
  - **Session-13 "no tombstone marker on VerbatimCard" lock is LIFTED.** Operator approved the chip during the design proposal. Ownership-phrase lock remains in place.
  - **Live HEAD verification did NOT run.** Dry-run on 5,061 URLs at workers=16 returned 4585 × `http_429` + 33 × `http_403` + 443 × `ok` (0 tombstone-eligible). Reddit dominates the corpus (4898 / 5061 = 96.8%) and rate-limits anonymous concurrent HEAD requests aggressively. Conservative rule meant a live run would have tombstoned 0 — same outcome as dry-run, no information gain. **Status: code shipped, live verification deferred behind reddit-domain throttling work** (probably its own ~1–2 hr bite using either authenticated reddit API or per-domain semaphore + sleep loop).
  - **The 33 × `http_403`s are likely retailer bot-blocks** (amazon / bestbuy), which our plain httpx HEAD doesn't get past. The catalog_discovery path uses `curl_cffi` Chrome120 impersonation for the same reason. Real link-rot detection on retailer URLs would need the same TLS-fingerprint path.
- **Live findings from session 38 (operator visibility):**
  - **96.8% of the corpus is reddit.** `SELECT source_type, COUNT(*) FROM mentions GROUP BY source_type ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC` → reddit_comment 4288, reddit_post 610, youtube_chunk 119, article 44. Any URL-health surface that doesn't handle reddit at scale handles less than 4% of the corpus.
  - **The dry-run's signal-to-noise was ~9% useful.** 443 ok / 5061 = 8.8%. At workers=4 limit=200 (slow throttle spot-check): 141 ok / 200 = 70.5% — slowing helps, but doesn't fully resolve reddit at full scale.
  - **Conservative rule is doing real work.** Without it, the 4585 × http_429 would have all turned into false-positive tombstones — wiping the entire reddit corpus from new aggregations. The "leave alive on transient / ambiguous" rule is what makes the verifier safe to ship before throttling lands.
  - **The session-13 lock-lift was clean.** No callers outside `VerbatimCard.tsx` and the fixture file needed updating; the `tombstone` chip tone was already defined in `Chip.tsx` (had been forward-looking). Lift was a one-line comment update + a 7-line JSX block addition.
  - **ARCH §9 doc-policy drift was the load-bearing find.** Original §9 said tombstoned mentions "remain contributors" — implementation choice (filter at aggregation) contradicts that. Per CLAUDE.md doc-evolution protocol, the deviation should have been flagged-then-doc'd during design; instead it surfaced during wrap-up. Updated §9 to match implementation.

  - **The diagnosis pass refuted its leading hypothesis cleanly.** Initial prediction (per session 31's "Sonnet over-labels Verdict prose") was that NotebookCheck entries would be over-represented in the disagreement set. Sample inspection of msi_cyborg_15 disagreement showed all 9 labeled aspects substantively discussed; Haiku's `pred` matched 8 of 9, gap was intensity wobble (display high→medium, performance high→medium, thermals medium→low).
  - **83 of 90 strict-tuple disagreements were intensity-only.** The strict metric was double-counting each wobble as both FP and FN. Loose metric collapsed all 83 to TP, accounting for the +82 TP delta observed in the re-eval (delta of one due to a single secondary-effect adjacency I didn't trace, but rounding error).
  - **Cache effectiveness on the re-eval.** Strict 0.5998 (session 31) → 0.6000 (session 37) — essentially zero drift, confirming the deterministic `(content_hash, prompt_version, model, temperature)` cache key. LLM cost on the re-eval: ~$0 (hot cache).
  - **Parser warnings during re-eval that the strict-metric was masking.** `aspect_classifier: unknown polarity 'mixed'` (1×); `duplicate aspect in response` (7×). Each drops a label from the classifier output, costing TP. Not pursued at Wave 2 closure but cited in the deferred Wave 4 polish note in TASKS.md — they're the cheapest path to closing the residual 0.022 gap if a future operator wants it.

- **Live findings from session 35 (operator visibility):**
  - **ASUS merge required a DB UPDATE, not just a YAML edit.** `/api/products` reads from the SQLite `products` table on every request via SQLAlchemy (`session.execute(select(Product))`), NOT from the YAML config. The YAML is consulted only at ingest time. After updating the YAML AND restarting uvicorn, the API still served `ASUS ROG` — root cause was 9 stale rows in the DB from the original ingest. Direct `UPDATE products SET brand='ASUS' WHERE brand='ASUS ROG'` resolved immediately, no uvicorn restart needed.
  - **Audit subagent false-positives on focus-rings + `prefers-reduced-motion`.** Both are wired globally in `frontend/src/index.css` via `*:focus-visible { box-shadow: var(--aw-focus-ring) }` and `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { animation-duration: 0ms; ... }`. Subagent grepped for per-element `focus:` Tailwind classes and missed the global rules. Per `feedback_audit_subagent_verify` memory, verified directly before patching.
  - **`verified_share` = 0.00 across all 489 aggregate rows under `run_wave5_v1`** because zero of the 5,061 ingested mentions carry a `verified_purchase` key in their `metadata` blob. Reddit (4,898) + YouTube chunks (119) + articles (44) — none of those source types expose a verified-purchase concept. Signal would unlock with BestBuy + Amazon retailer-review ingestion (Wave 2 deferred plumbing).
  - **No backend / schema / LLM changes this session.** $0 LLM spend; cumulative across sessions remains ~$19.70.

  1. **Operator brief-review walk.** Open each of the 53 standalone briefs in `/standalone/:id` and read them as a manufacturer exec would — do the claims read true? Are the strengths/weaknesses the right ones? Do the quotes support each claim? Flag anything wrong, weak, or surprising. Output = a fixes list for the next bite. This is the pilot's actual output-aha gate per `feedback_capability_vs_output_aha` memory. No new code; operator-driven session.
  2. **Reproducibility re-run.** Re-run the Stage A + Stage B pipeline on the same corpus; cache should hit on every LLM call; resulting briefs + aggregates should be byte-identical to what's already in the DB. Validates the "byte-identical re-run" promise. ~30 min, $0 LLM if caching works.
  3. **Stakeholder prep packet.** Screenshots of all three pages on real data, one-page narrative ("here's what we built, here's what it found, here's the cost, here's monthly-refresh cadence"), talking points. Optional but is the pilot exit deliverable.
  4. **(Optional) Pair brief workflow.** Would unlock the existing `BriefExportButton` on `/pair` (component is already generic). Bigger scope — needs a new pair brief Sonnet prompt + persistence + retrieval endpoint.
  5. **(Optional) Sonnet aspect-labeler iteration.** Tighten labeler to avoid NotebookCheck Verdict-paragraph over-labeling; rebuild `aspect_tagging_v3` gold; re-eval Haiku. Could close the formal F1 ≥0.80 gate. ~$2-5.

Globs `directory.glob("*.yaml")` — top-level only; sub-directories are honored as quarantine. - **Operator-confirmed locks from session 28 (do not re-debate without flag):**
  - **REVERSAL of session-27 D-lock "model alone is sufficient."** Session-27 verification was a single-product probe on ROG Strix G16 (specific name with unique "Strix" token); at 59-product scale, generic queries like `Lenovo Legion 9i` / `MSI Vector 18` / `ASUS TUF Gaming 14` / `Acer Nitro 15` all return 500-cap results that are mostly off-target (tablets, phones, unrelated laptops). Notebookcheck's POST `model` field OR-tokenizes on common words. Manufacturer-dropdown extension is the next-corpus-expansion lever.
  - **Discovery output is bimodal at 59-product scale.** 14 zero-coverage (product not on Notebookcheck under the display_name) + 30 clean (1-2 editorials each) + 14 mega-noise (500-cap garbage) + 1 transient timeout (msi_vector_18 — was also mega-noise on retry). Real curation surface = ~30-60 editorials, matching session-27's 30-100 estimate once mega-noise is excluded.

Interactive CLI still deferred.

  - **Date filter default = `2024-09-01` (~14-month window)** for the 2025-26 generation universe. Drops `published_at is None` too. Easy per-run override.
  - **500-cap is Notebookcheck's response truncation wall**, not a date-feed fallback (probe `ZZZQQQ_NONEXISTENT_LAPTOP` → 0 results, confirms the search IS filtering). Hitting 500 is a useful signal that the query is too loose; treat the YAML as suspect.

  - **Bite 10.4 — citation validator + orchestrator + CLI + first live smoke (session 12):**
    - **`pulse_check/synthesis/citation_validator.py`** (full rewrite) — `validate_citations(session, *, narrative, aggregates, allowed_pool, drift_tolerance=0.05) -> ValidationResult`. Four soft-warn checks per TESTING §6: (1) **Fabricated IDs** — cited_id NOT in `mentions` table (queries DB once per call). (2) **Out-of-context IDs** — cited_id in DB but NOT in `allowed_pool` (= PRIMARY ∪ SECONDARY mention_ids the orchestrator passed to selector). (3) **Numerical drift** — regex `\b(\d+)\s+(?:user|mention|thread|reviewer|comment|post|review|owner|customer|complaint|complain|praise|report)s?\b` (case-insensitive) catches count-noun patterns; compares each integer to the cited aggregate's mention count for the matched bucket (PRIMARY for §1/§2, SECONDARY for §3/§4) via `_resolve_actual_n` — flagged if `|claimed - actual| / max(actual, 1) > drift_tolerance`. (4) **Empty claims** — claim with empty cited_mention_ids whose claim_text doesn't equal the §6.3 placeholder constant. Returns `ValidationResult` with `is_valid = not (any of the 4 lists are non-empty)`. **Soft-warn — never raises.** Signature deviates from 10.1 skeleton: added `aggregates` param (drift needs counts), renamed `primary_pool` → `allowed_pool` (brief cites both buckets per §6.3 four-quadrant lock).
    - **`pulse_check/synthesis/brief_writer.py`** — added public `BRIEF_MODEL = "claude-sonnet-4-6"` (alias of internal `_SONNET_MODEL`) + `BRIEF_PROMPT_VERSION_STRICT = "a1_brief_v1_strict"` + `_SONNET_PROMPT_STRICT_PREAMBLE` (3-bullet preamble emphasizing fidelity to provided verbatims, no count fabrication, no aspect/quadrant fabrication) + `_BRIEF_PROMPT_TEMPLATES` dispatch dict registering both versions. `write_a1_brief` body now does `prompt_template = _BRIEF_PROMPT_TEMPLATES[prompt_version]` (raises `ValueError` on unknown version) and uses `prompt_template.format(...)` instead of `_SONNET_PROMPT.format(...)`. Public signature unchanged.
    - **`pulse_check/synthesis/orchestrator.py`** (full rewrite) — `synthesize_a1(session, *, client, run_id, product_id) -> Brief`. Stages: (1) load Product (raises `ValueError` if missing); (2) load aggregates by `(product_id, run_id)` (raises if zero rows); (3) build `allowed_pool = primary_ids ∪ secondary_ids`; (4) `cluster_near_duplicates` on PRIMARY mentions (skipped when pool is empty); (5) loop selector per aspect; (6) `write_a1_brief` with `prompt_version=BRIEF_PROMPT_VERSION`; (7) `validate_citations`; (8) **on `fabricated_ids` only — one retry with `prompt_version=BRIEF_PROMPT_VERSION_STRICT` + revalidate** (other warning channels pass straight through); (9) persist `Brief` row with `narrative_dict = narrative.model_dump() | {"flagged_citation_issues": result.model_dump()}` injected as top-level key, `scope_type=ASPECT_1_SKU`, `scope_id=product_id`, `prompt_version=used_prompt_version`, `model=BRIEF_MODEL`. Signature deviates from 10.1 skeleton: added `client: AnthropicClient` param (brief writer + dedup require it).

Persists `Brief` to DB (NOT a markdown file — operator-flagged at session close). Returns `1` on `ValueError` (missing product / no aggregates), `2` on missing `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `0` otherwise (soft-warn).

## Things to verify when next session resumes

Manual checks the prior session couldn't / didn't do, listed so they don't get lost:

- **Aspect classifier anchors are synthetic** — 22 short verbatims approximating gaming-laptop review language. v2 anchor refinement still expected after first eval iteration; not yet executed.
- **Sonnet gold labeling reuses the Qwen prompt verbatim** — same label space (deliberate). If Sonnet labels look weak during operator spot-check, consider a richer Sonnet-specific prompt as a separate bite.
- **scrapers-lib BestBuy URL HTML-fallback path is unit-tested but never hit production (session 7).** Both BestBuy URLs in session 7 timed out at the network layer before any HTML was returned; the new HTML-fallback regex (`"skuId":"<7d>"` via `_html_unescape`) is verified against the AREA51 fixture but unverified against live Strix HTML. Will revisit when bite 6.4 returns.

## Open items to revisit (deferred, not lost)

Carrying forward from session 1:
- **Tombstoning retention policy** — exact retention window + UI badge wording. Defer to implementation.
- **Demo pair plan breadth** — currently all 10 Alienware-vs-competitor pairs; operator may want to narrow to ~5 segment-matched pairs. Easy toggle in pair-plan config.
- **Anchor attribution regex patterns** — refine iteratively during first scrape pass. Live in `configs/product_set_*.yaml` (no code change required; scrapers-lib wrappers auto-prepend `re:` prefix).
- **BestBuy API key** — not blocking for v1; Tier 3 paginated reviews cover the review signal without it.
- **Alienware display font licensing** — fallback chain Eurostile → Exo 2 → Rajdhani in place per DESIGN_SYSTEM §8.3 (Exo 2 + Rajdhani loaded from Google Fonts in `frontend/index.html`); revisit if the brand font becomes licensable.
- **YouTube + article seed lists** — parallel prereq task (curate ~50–75 YouTube URLs + ~150–200 article URLs for the 10-pair demo); needed by Wave 5.

Carrying forward from session 2:
- **ProductSnapshot table (v1.5?)** — scrapers-lib's Amazon product fetcher emits `ProductSnapshot` (price, rating, review_count, specs). v1 ingester skips and counts them. If price tracking becomes in-scope, add a snapshot table + ingest branch.
- **Reddit thread-level fetching** — Wave 3 deliberation decoder needs `fetch_reddit_comments` on individual threads; orchestrator currently only enqueues `fetch_reddit_listing` per subreddit. Wave 3 will add thread-discovery + thread-fetch enqueueing.

Added in session 3:
- **Aspect classifier prompt v2 anchor refresh** — after the first real Qwen pass + eval, expect to refine the 22 synthetic anchors with real-language patterns. Bumping `PROMPT_VERSION = "aspect_classifier_v2"` cold-starts the cache; old `_v1` rows stay as audit trail.
- **Sonnet-specific gold-set prompt** — currently Sonnet uses the Qwen prompt verbatim. Revisit if operator spot-check shows Sonnet labels are weak; richer prompt would live alongside as `aspect_classifier_sonnet_v1`.
- **`scripts/serve.py` Windows job-control** — uses `subprocess.terminate()` which is a soft kill; on Windows this sends Ctrl+Break to console children, not necessarily to grandchildren (npm → node). If serve.py leaves orphaned vite processes, switch to `subprocess.Popen(creationflags=CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP)` + `os.kill(pid, signal.CTRL_BREAK_EVENT)`. Watch for it next session.
- **`API_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` env parsing footgun** — pydantic-settings parses `list[str]` from env as JSON, so the `.env` value must be `["http://localhost:5173"]` not `http://localhost:5173`. Documented in `.env.example` but easy to mis-edit.
- **Cache key for the classifier excludes display_name** — by design (rename without retag), but means changing only `display_name` in a product config doesn't reflect in cached labels. Acceptable for the rename case; flag if surprising.

Added in session 4:
- **`aggregates_aspect_sku` schema lacks version columns** — uniqueness is `(run_id, product_id, aspect)` only. Re-aggregating against a different `(taxonomy_version, prompt_version)` overwrites prior rows. Acceptable as "current pass" semantics; if version-stratified aggregates are ever needed, add `taxonomy_version` + `prompt_version` columns + extend uniqueness key (Alembic migration). Flagged in-conversation when bite 5 was implemented.
- **Subagent file-creation blocked in this harness** — Write/Bash mkdir denied for background subagents (saved as memory `feedback_subagent_write.md`). Code-writing tasks must run in foreground; use subagents for read-only research only. Watch if permissions change.

Added in session 5:
- **Review-vs-deal pre-classifier** — operator's session-5 finding from spot-check. Anchor regex matches deal-roundup posts where the product appears in a list among 10+ others (e.g. "Black Friday Gaming Laptop Deals under $1100"). Result: ~70% of `/top?t=year` corpus is promotional noise rather than substantive opinion. Future fix: a Haiku pre-pass classifying each mention as `review | deal | other` and dropping non-reviews before tagging. Cheap (~$0.001/mention) and high-leverage. Operator-flagged at session-5 close. **Resolved in session 6 — bite 6.1 built the gate; 65% deal contamination confirmed empirically.**
- **LLM cache writes share the SQLAlchemy session** — when the session rolls back on exception, cache rows roll back too. We saw this twice in session 5 (Qwen tag crash → cache lost → re-run did everything from scratch). Cache should run on its own connection/transaction so partial work survives crashes.
- **`tag_corpus_aspects` partial-progress preservation** — currently flushes only at end. Hard crash before circuit-breaker trips loses all in-memory work. Per-N-mention commit (e.g. every 10) bounds the loss; modest contract change ("caller no longer fully owns the transaction").
- **Batch dedup is model-agnostic** — `already_tagged` pre-pass filters on `(taxonomy_version, prompt_version)` only. Switching providers (Ollama → Anthropic) creates rows under the same `prompt_version` that block re-tagging. Three options: extend the pre-pass filter to include `model`; OR bump `prompt_version` when switching providers; OR delete prior rows on provider change. Defer until provider switching is a routine workflow.
- **`OLLAMA_MODEL` default unpin** — `.env.example` pins `qwen2.5:7b-q4_K_M` (specific quantization). Ollama's default `ollama pull qwen2.5:7b` returns a different quant (q4_0). Mismatch caused HTTP 404 in session 5. Either unpin to `qwen2.5:7b` in `.env.example` or document `ollama pull qwen2.5:7b-q4_K_M` as a setup step.
- **`tests/unit/scraping/test_orchestrator.py`** — agent-designed during session 5 (3 cases: insert, idempotent rerun, display_name update). Patch 1 (production) applied; Patch 2 (test) deferred to avoid debug distraction during smoke. Apply early next session — it locks in the upsert behavior with regression coverage.

Added in session 6:
- **A1 aggregator does not honor content-type filter** — bite 6.1's `--exclude-content-types` gate operates at aspect-tag time. `aggregate_a1` rolls up ALL `aspect_tags` rows under `(taxonomy_version, prompt_version)` regardless of mention content-type. Validating that filtering changes the brief shape requires either re-running aspect tagging on a deal-cleaned corpus (destructive, since existing aspect_tags rows would need to be deleted first) OR teaching the aggregator a content_type filter (proposed bite 6.1.5). Likely subsumed by 6.2 corpus expansion; revisit if not.
- **Content-type prompt v1 misclassifies defect reports as `other`** — the screen-flashing post (Path A's most actionable finding) was first-person ownership + clear evaluation, but framed as a help-request. Anchor examples didn't cover defect-report-shaped reviews, so it landed in `other`. Practical mitigation: the right gate invocation is `--exclude-content-types deal` only (keep review AND other). v2 prompt with a defect-report anchor is a future option; premature at n=31.
- **Ruff lint slipped past pre-commit** — `alembic/versions/fa194da18ea1_initial_schema.py` had an I001 import-order error that session 5's commit claimed clean. Auto-fixed at session-6 audit start. Pre-commit setup may not have been active when that file was authored; not investigating further unless it recurs.
- **TASKS.md was stale through session 5** — every checkbox `[ ]` despite Wave 1 being done and Wave 2 ~60%. Reconciled at session-6 audit start; "Last reconciled" line added to header. If future sessions don't update at close, will drift again — flag the discrepancy when it shows up.
- **`runs` table is empty despite aggregates_aspect_sku referencing run_id** — session 5 produced 17 aggregate rows with run_ids that have no matching `runs` row. Foreign keys are not enforced by SQLite by default. Not blocking; flag if a future bite assumes runs/aggregates are joinable.
- **Capability vs output aha distinction** — saved as `feedback_capability_vs_output_aha.md` (memory). Path A confirmed the capability holds; output aha requires corpus density. Apply as evaluation lens at every architectural milestone going forward.

Added in session 7:
- ~~**scrapers-lib `_version.py` deferred bump.**~~ **RESOLVED at session-9 audit start** — `_version.py` is `1.2.1` in HEAD with no working-tree diff; the bump was committed (not by us, between sessions 8 and 9). Earlier session-9 messages still reference "1.2.0 in HEAD with edit to 1.2.1" — that framing is stale.
- **pulse-check `result_sink` does not map `('amazon', 'post')` or `('bestbuy', 'post')`.** Discovered in session 7 when Amazon Alienware fetched cleanly (HTTP 200) but ingestion rejected the row. Latent issue — these mappings were never exercised before. Fix is a small extension (10–30 lines + test); parked in bite 6.4.
- **Amazon Strix returned HTTP 200 but parser found zero inline reviews.** Possible causes: (a) anti-bot stripped page, (b) scrapers-lib selectors stale for this product layout, (c) reviews behind a "see all reviews" link the parser doesn't follow. Diagnose in bite 6.4.
- **BestBuy reachability from this machine.** Both URLs hit curl 28 timeout × 2 → 1h domain backoff (in scrapers-lib scheduler). Could be local network, regional IP, or Akamai escalation. Diagnose with manual curl outside scrapers-lib (and ideally from a different network) when bite 6.4 returns. Backoff auto-expires 1h after last attempt.
- **Run config `paginate: false` for BestBuy.** Smoke config gives only ~5 PDP-embedded reviews per product. Switch to `paginate: true` in `configs/run_smoke_test.yaml` (and `configs/run_demo_2026_04.yaml`) when bite 6.4 wants real density via BestBuy.

Added in session 8:
- **A1 aggregator is PRIMARY-only by design — surfaces as a discoverability gap.** Comment-inheritance produces a 565-row SECONDARY-attributed aspect_tags corpus, and A1's PRIMARY-only filter makes that work invisible at the aggregate layer. Option 3 (dual-track PRIMARY/SECONDARY columns) is the agreed fix. Sub-decisions: (a) which fields to dual-track (4 baseline vs 8 full parity); (b) naming (`*_secondary` suffix vs nested JSON); (c) whether to expose `total_mentions_combined` as a code-computed virtual.
- **`aggregate_a1` arithmetic doc lives only in code, not ARCHITECTURE.** §7.1 step 1 reads "whose `mention_id` is attributed to P" — silent on primary-vs-secondary. Option 3 implementation requires updating §3.3 (column inventory) AND §7.1 (algorithm) to make the dual-track explicit.
- **Comment-inheritance is a new attribution mechanism not described in ARCHITECTURE §5.** Currently §5 covers only "primary at fetch time" + "secondary post-fetch raw_text regex sweep". The parent-post inheritance path is a third mechanism (parent-link-based, no regex on the comment text). ARCHITECTURE §5 needs a third paragraph (drafted in session-8 wrap, ready to paste).
- **scrapers-lib `_version.py` 1.1.0 unbumped, now with two pending releases worth of edits.** Session 7's BestBuy URL extension + session 8's `emit_all_comments` both sit under `[Unreleased]/Added`. Whoever cuts the next release picks the version (likely 1.2.0).
- **content_type breakdown ratios shifted at scale.** At n=31 (session 6) the corpus was ~65% deal. At n=1143 (session 8) it's 4% deal / 87% other / 9% review. The deal-roundup contamination characteristic of `/top?t=year` listings is diluted by the comment corpus, which lands almost entirely in `other`. Re-validate the `--exclude-content-types deal` gate behavior on the new scale before relying on session-6 framing.
- **`apply_comment_inheritance` matches parents only via `metadata_["parent_id"] == "t3_<post_id>"`.** Comments whose parent post is not in our DB are skipped silently (intended). Watch for unexpected 0-inheritance counts on future scrapes — likely indicates a schema/format change in the parent_id metadata.
- **Aspect classifier is now Haiku not Qwen — undocumented in ARCHITECTURE §6.** Per session-5 swap. ARCHITECTURE §6.1 needs a deviation note + §6.5 Haiku batch-classifiers entry covering both `tag_mention_aspects` (Haiku since session 5) and `classify_content_type` (Haiku since session 6).

Added in session 16:
- **AspectColumn sticky-inside-viewport pattern (cross-platform alignment fix).** When a sticky column header sits OUTSIDE a scroll viewport, the viewport reserves ~15px for the scrollbar (Windows) and rows shift left while the outside header keeps full width — visible drift between header text and row values. Cross-platform fix: put the column header INSIDE the scroll viewport with `sticky top-0 z-20`; sub-headers stick at `top-[N]px z-10` where N = column header height (~28px in our 11px-text + py-1.5 styling). Both share width context, alignment is exact regardless of OS scrollbar width. Future tables with sticky headers under sub-headers should reach for this pattern by default.
- **Per-row bucket chip kept inline even within Primary/Secondary sub-sections.** Sticky sub-header names the bucket (Primary signal / Secondary signal) so the per-row chip is technically redundant in those sections. Kept anyway because (a) Long-tail mixes buckets and the chip is essential there, and (b) a uniform AspectRow API beats conditional chip rendering by section. If the visual gets noisy, conditionalize before refactoring.
- **`partitionAspects` cross-scroller divergence is hot path for Standalone correctness.** Top-3 PRIMARY uses `total_mentions/net_sentiment` for polarity placement; top-3 SECONDARY uses `total_mentions_secondary/net_sentiment_secondary` and EXCLUDES aspects already in this column's Primary; Long-tail uses PRIMARY-precedence to pick which bucket's metrics to show. An aspect with PRIMARY pos and SECONDARY neg correctly appears in left-Primary AND right-Secondary with different metrics + chips. 11.3.b/c will hit the same logic against live aggregates — confirm the live `total_mentions_secondary` numbers match the partition expectations on visual confirm.
- **DESIGN_SYSTEM §9 still has 4 open visual questions for 11.3 build:** (1) CiteChip exact wording (`[3 mentions]` vs `3·` etc); (2) Sparkline 26-week series source (compute-at-handler vs extend aggregator); (3) Source coverage display (5 dots vs source marks); (4) Long-tail visual weight (muted vs identical to Primary/Secondary). All build-time calls during 11.3.b/c. Operator can decide each in-iteration; flag if any blocks structural progress.
- **Operator visual iteration cadence within a single bite is high.** 5 visual tweaks landed in 11.3.a alone (max-h, column header restore, sub-header chip drop, N-ids drop, intensity drop, alignment fix). This is the normal `/#/showcase` review pattern — expect similar in 11.3.b. Don't over-engineer the first cut; operator's eye is the calibration loop.
- **Subagent design-then-parent-saves split worked clean again** (validates `feedback_subagent_write.md`). One general-purpose agent read 7 files + designed the full 11.3.a refactor + returned verbatim code in fences; parent applied via 2 Writes + 6 Edits. Same pattern recommended for 11.3.b's pages + router work if it crosses many files.

DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.2 now mandates rendering `narrative.sections[i].heading` as-returned, mapping to quadrants positionally (sections[0]=Q1, [1]=Q2, [2]=Q3, [3]=Q4). Frontend during 11.2/11.3 must respect this — do NOT hardcode section titles.

Added in session 18:
- **🔥 Pre-existing Wave 1 BASE_URL bug, surfaced ONLY by headless smoke.** `frontend/src/lib/api.ts` used `??` to fall back BASE_URL to `"./api"`, but `.env.production` sets `VITE_API_URL=` (empty string). `??` only triggers on null/undefined — empty string is "defined" — so the production bundle ended up with `BASE_URL=""`, every fetch hit `/products` (etc.) instead of `/api/products`, got caught by the SPA fallback, returned `<!doctype …>`, and `JSON.parse` threw `"Unexpected token '<'"`. Bug had existed since the Wave 1 stub but was first exercised end-to-end during 11.3.d smoke (Showcase never fetched, and Vite dev never read `.env.production`). One-char fix (`??` → `||`) + comment locking the intent. **Generalized lesson:** any env-driven fallback should use `||`, not `??`, when an empty string should also trigger the default — `.env` files routinely produce empty strings.
- **Headless-Playwright smoke is a high-leverage practice and should be the default closer for any bite that wires live API calls or routing.** Build dist (`npm run build`) → uvicorn auto-mounts the SPA from `frontend/dist` → drive headless Chromium through every route → capture `console` / `pageerror` / `requestfailed` / `request` / `response` events → take a `full_page=True` screenshot per route. This caught the BASE_URL bug that unit tests + mypy + ruff + tsc all passed clean against. Smoke script lives at `C:/Users/AW-testing/AppData/Local/Temp/smoke_11_3_c.py` — disposable; rewrite per bite. Pattern worth memory: see `feedback_e2e_smoke_pattern.md` (if added).
- **Stale process squatting on the dev API port** — first instance of operator-machine state breaking a bite. `python -m uvicorn app.main:app --port 8765` (different project; module `app.main` not `pulse_check.api.main`) had been running on the operator's machine before session start. Audit `netstat -ano | grep ":8765"` + `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "ProcessId = <pid>" | Select CommandLine` at session start; if CommandLine isn't `pulse_check.api.main:app`, stop it before bite work and replace with the venv one. Killing required explicit operator approval per CLAUDE.md "Executing actions with care."
- **Two `.env` files with subtly different intent.** `.env.development` points Vite (5173) at the running uvicorn (`VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:8765/api`); `.env.production` is empty (`VITE_API_URL=`) to let `BASE_URL` fall back to `./api` for the unified-server case. Empty isn't a typo — it's a load-bearing signal that the BASE_URL guard MUST handle (see BASE_URL bug above).
- **Backend deviation: `ProductDetail.latest_brief_id: int | None` added** (single-field response addition, not a route change). Driven by Standalone needing to navigate product → brief in one hop without a sibling lookup endpoint. Backend handler queries `MAX(brief_id) WHERE scope_type == ASPECT_1_SKU AND scope_id == product_id`. Frontend mirrors as `number | null`. **Pattern**: prefer the additive-field route when the alternative is a new endpoint or a frontend mapping table.
- **Native `<select>` shipped vs DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.6's custom dropdown.** YAGNI for pilot scale (2 brands, 2 products). Callsite contract is identical to a future custom impl (`{label, value, options, onChange}`), so swapping later is cheap. Flagged in §5.6 with the deviation note. Revisit if option-row meta text (`2,847 mentions` etc.) becomes a real need.
- **Optimistic-open pattern with stale-guard for lazy async panels.** Both EvidenceDrawer and CitationPanel open immediately on user click + render `Loading evidence…` / `Loading citations…` while the `/api/mentions` request is in flight. When the response resolves, the page-level state setter **checks that the panel is still showing the same aspect/claim** before merging in the mentions — otherwise a second click would have stomped the first fetch (or worse, the first fetch would arrive late and clobber the second click's payload). Standalone uses this for both panels; saved as a useful component-contract addition (`loading?: boolean` + `errorMessage?: string | null`) that's also backward-compatible (Showcase still passes neither).
- **Live brief headings (`"High-confidence strengths"` etc.) AND fixture headings (`"What's working (PRIMARY)"` etc.) both route correctly with negative-first keyword priority.** Order matters: `"not working"` / `"weakness"` checked first. A heading combining both tokens would route to negative — the safer mis-classification (red dot instead of dropped claim). Live curl confirmed all 8 sections (4 per brief × 2 briefs) route to the right bucket. Sonnet is unlikely to produce ambiguous headings under T=0 + locked prompt, but the negative-first ordering is the right defensive choice.
- **Operator visual smoke remains the final gate, even with headless smoke green.** Headless catches programmatic regressions (BASE_URL, 404 routing, console errors); it doesn't catch visual taste / spacing / token drift. Session 18 ended with 13/13 headless checks green + screenshots reviewed inline with operator; full operator browser walk still pending until session 19 (or earlier ad-hoc).

Added in session 23:
- **🔥 Deliberation classifier contract gap: "OP picked externally" conflated with "unresolved".** Surfaced via operator review of Entry 1 (`reddit_post_1nf64o8`). Current `deliberation_classifier_v1` constrains `chosen_product_id` to the tracked product universe (per prompt rule 4 + `parse_response`'s `_coerce_chosen_product_id`). When OP picks an out-of-universe product (Entry 1 chose Lenovo Legion 15 Pro), the classifier returns `is_resolved=False, chosen=null` — **structurally indistinguishable** from "OP didn't decide". For A2 `pair_win_rates`, this loses a real signal: tracked products LOST this deliberation. **Fix path (deferred):** `deliberation_classifier_v2` — bump `PROMPT_VERSION`; add `chosen_external_name: str | None` field to `DeliberationPrediction`; relax classifier rule so `is_resolved=True` when OP names ANY product (in-universe → `chosen_product_id`; out-of-universe → `chosen_external_name`). ARCH §6.1 row edit + parser changes + test updates. Captured in TASKS.md as a new Wave 3 line. For session-23's gold-set entry, operator override is encoded in `operator_labels` as a flexible-dict ad-hoc key (`chosen_external_name="Lenovo Legion 15 Pro"`) — no schema migration needed for the gold set.
- **Compare-page UI scope undecided — Source A vs Source B (or both).** Surfaced via operator-articulated architectural question during Entry 4 review. Pulse-check has TWO sources of comparative signal: **Source A** = deliberation decoder (`pair_win_rates` + `aggregates_pair_reason` from resolved deliberation threads) — Wave 3 territory; **Source B** = cross-product aspect-sentiment diff derived from A1 `aggregates_aspect_sku` (computable at UI/API time, no new pipeline). v1 ships A1 + Source B (the Compare page implicitly already has it via per-aspect A vs B diffs); Wave 3 A2 adds Source A. Open question: should the Compare page render Source A only, Source B only, or both side-by-side? PRD-level decision. Likely both, but flagged for explicit operator + design call before Wave 3 frontend work starts. No code-side change pending; just a doc/decision item.
- **Sampler dedup-by-Reddit-post-id missing.** Live run produced 2 deliberation-gold entries for the same Reddit post (`1nvihfg` — "Ultimate Buying Guide") under two different anchor-distinguished mention_ids (`_alienware_16_aurora` and `_rog_strix_g16`). Cache hit deduped the Sonnet call (no double-spend); JSONL still emitted both entries. For an eval gold set, this inflates per-post weight on the test suite. **Fix path (cheap):** in `pulse_check/eval/sampler.py select_candidate_thread_ids`, dedup by Reddit `post_id` (via `_post_id_from_post_mention_id`) before sorted-union → keep one mention_id per post (lowest mention_id by sort order, preserving determinism). One function change + 1-2 tests. Parked as session-24 Path C.
- **Operator-driven review flow: in-chat one-at-a-time vs interactive CLI.** Session 23's review of the 7 deliberation entries ran via in-chat AskUserQuestion calls (per operator's "you do it, one at a time to mark a or q") rather than the operator running `scripts/review_deliberation_gold_set.py` directly. Marks were applied programmatically at the end via a Python helper using `read_deliberation_gold_jsonl` + `write_deliberation_gold_jsonl`. This pattern worked smoothly at N=7 but doesn't scale — at N=40+ (eventual Wave 5 reason gold), operator should run the CLI directly. The CLI is built + tested + functional; just not used in session 23. Flagged as a pattern preference, not a bug.
- **Zero-resolved-to-tracked-product corpus density.** V1 corpus (33 reddit_post mentions) produced **0 resolved-to-tracked-product deliberations** across 7 candidates that passed the (b) ∪ (c) heuristic. Two of seven were Rule-B "deliberating but no in-universe winner" (Entry 1 chose Lenovo externally; Entry 7 OP undecided). The reason gold set is therefore empty by structural fact, not classifier failure. **Operator-accepted that Wave 5 corpus expansion is THE unblock** (vs loosening heuristic or relaxing the classifier — both rejected as not addressing the underlying corpus density issue). This is the rare-event-play warning from memory `feedback_pilot_focus` made concrete: A2 / Source A findings need a denser corpus to surface.

**Decisions (product-level) reached this session.**
- **D1 — `refresh.py` defaults to dry-run; `--execute` opts in.** Bare invocation prints the plan + cost estimate + due verdict but fires no subprocess. The opposite default (execute by default with `--dry-run` opt-in) was the alternate fork. Operator picked dry-run-default because the scheduler will invoke this script unattended, and the misfire cost of an unattended `--execute` is $5–25 of LLM credit per quarterly cycle.
- **D2 — Windows Task Scheduler fires dry-run only.** The XML template invokes `refresh_quarterly.ps1` which calls `refresh.py` WITHOUT `--execute`. The scheduled job's role is to produce a dry-run plan in `data/refresh_log/dryrun_<ts>.log`; the operator reviews and manually runs `--execute` if the plan looks right. Alternative considered: full auto-execute with `--no-confirm` flag. Operator rejected on safety grounds — losing pre-flight review for an unattended LLM-spend operation is a worse trade than the manual touchpoint.
- **D3 — Spec tokens in DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §2.7 + §3.2 are aspirational, not load-bearing.** Bite #8 audit surfaced that `--aw-space-*` / `--aw-line-tight/normal/loose` / `--aw-weight-*` tokens are declared in spec but never appear in any code file. Two forks: (a) annotate spec as aspirational (no code changes), (b) wire tokens into `index.css` + `tailwind.config.ts` and migrate components (multi-file refactor). Operator picked (a). Rationale: today's Tailwind-default spacing has been fine in shipped reality through sessions 11–39; no functional gain from wiring the tokens until consistency drift actually shows up.
- **D4 — Markdown brief export ships as a Download action, not Copy-to-Clipboard.** Default chosen for parity with the existing Download-HTML toolbar button (which is the closest analog). The markdown string lives in-memory in `handleMarkdownDownload`; a future bite can branch the same string into `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` if paste-into-Slack workflows become a stakeholder ask.
- **D5 — Refresh state lives in a JSON file, not a DB column.** Did not add a `Run.last_refresh_at` SQLAlchemy column / migration. State is single-row, mutable on success, atomic on write, doesn't need referential integrity. Promote to a `RefreshLog` table if/when cross-run history becomes a product surface (e.g., "show last 5 refreshes on the About page"). YAGNI for the skeleton.

### 2026-05-20 — session 38: bite #5 link-rot / tombstoning — code shipped, live verification deferred (new `pulse_check/verifiers/link_verifier.py` + `scripts/verify_mention_links.py` + aggregation tombstone-exclusion + `MentionView.tombstoned_at` + VerbatimCard "link dead" chip; ARCH §9 "remain contributors" policy REVERSED to "excluded from new aggregations"; dry-run on 5,061 URLs returned 90.6% http_429 from reddit rate-limiting, conservative rule yielded 0 tombstones, live HEAD batch not run; session-13 lock on tombstone marker lifted).

**Context entering.** Session 37 closed with bite #5 next in the polish queue (Wave 4 line 108 carry). Operator's starter prompt was the standard "audit first per SESSION_LOG · ultrathink · subagents · concise". Audit pass came back GREEN (17/17 checks).

**Audit pass (session 37 → 38).** GREEN via foreground subagent. 635/635 pytest unit · 2/2 integration · mypy clean 126 src · ruff clean · migration head `eb05da255474` · DB counts unchanged · eval JSON has both strict 0.5999 + loose 0.7783 · bundle `index-CkZUKoNo.js` · ARCH §6.6 retraction paragraph + DESIGN §4.9 Skeleton atom + PRD §7 loose-F1 line + TESTING §4 / §5.3 updates + README L187 loose headline all verified on disk. No drift.

**Decisions (product-level) reached this session.**
- **D1 — Bite #5 ships in two phases: code now, live verification deferred.** The bite as planned was (a) HEAD-check verifier, (b) tombstone-on-conservative-failure, (c) aggregation filter, (d) UI badge, (e) live HEAD batch on the 5,061 URLs. (a)–(d) shipped with 16 new tests + clean gates. (e) ran as DRY-RUN only and returned 4585 × http_429 + 33 × http_403 + 443 × ok. Conservative rule (only 404/410/dns/refused tombstone) meant a live run would have written exactly zero tombstones — same outcome as dry-run, no information gain. Operator accepted "code shipped, live verification deferred behind reddit-domain throttling work" as the bite's closure.
- **D2 — Conservative tombstone rule.** Only `http_404` / `http_410` / `dns_fail` / `conn_refused` write `tombstoned_at`. 403 / 5xx / timeout / generic transport errors leave the mention alive. Rationale: false-positive tombstoning is irreversible and removes citations from briefs; a single bad day for an upstream host or a retailer bot-block must not look like deletion. The dry-run validated this — 4585 reddit rate-limits would have wiped the entire reddit corpus from aggregates otherwise.
- **D3 — Aggregation filter is forward-only.** Implementation choice: filter `Mention.tombstoned_at IS NULL` in the aggregator's mention-pool fetch, AND drop aspect tags whose backing mention is tombstoned (the existing `_compute_bucket` would otherwise still increment polarity/intensity for missing mentions). Effect: applies to new runs only; existing `run_wave5_v1` aggregates are frozen by their baked-in `mention_ids` arrays — no retroactive surgery on prior briefs. Operator approved during the design proposal. **Caveat: this REVERSES the prior ARCH §9 policy "tombstoned mentions remain contributors to aggregates."** Updated §9 to match implementation; per CLAUDE.md doc-evolution protocol the deviation should have been flagged-then-doc'd during design but surfaced at wrap-up — recording the path for next-time discipline.
- **D4 — Session-13 lock on "no tombstone marker on VerbatimCard" lifted.** Operator approved the "link dead" chip during the design proposal. The `tombstone` chip tone was already defined in `Chip.tsx` (forward-looking placement from session 13). One JSX block added to tag row 2; one component-header comment updated; one Chip-tone-table doc line updated. Ownership-phrase lock retained.
- **D5 — Live HEAD batch deferred. Three-fork choice resolved as "park live, ship code, move to next bite."** Forks considered: (1) run live with bad signal (0 tombstones produced; no information gain), (2) iterate verifier first to add reddit-domain throttling (1–2 hr scope creep on a polish bite), (3) ship code + defer live behind a future throttling bite. Operator chose (3). Reddit-throttling becomes a deferred bite-#5-follow-on in the session-39 queue.

**Technical housekeeping (operator does not engage).**
- **TH1 — `verify_url(client, url)`.** HEAD-first with GET-stream fallback on 405. Follows redirects via httpx `follow_redirects=True`. Realistic Chrome 120 User-Agent string so retailers behave as they did at scrape time. 10 s read timeout, 5 s connect timeout via `httpx.Timeout(10.0, connect=5.0)`. Returns `VerifyResult(url, tombstone_reason, note)`; `note` is a short status tag for operator logs ("ok", "http_404", "timeout", "dns_fail", "conn_refused", "conn_error", "transport_error", "http_NNN").
- **TH2 — `_classify_connect_error(exc)`.** Walks `exc.__cause__ or exc.__context__` chain (cycle-guarded by id-set) looking for `socket.gaierror` (→ dns_fail) and `ConnectionRefusedError` (→ conn_refused). Generic `httpx.ConnectError` without a known socket cause returns `None` → falls through to `note="conn_error"` and stays alive. Tested via `raise httpx.ConnectError(...) from socket.gaierror(...)` pattern in the MockTransport handler.
- **TH3 — `verify_all_pending(session, client, *, limit, workers=16, commit_every=100, dry_run=False)`.** Snapshots `(mention_id, source_url)` tuples from the DB first (not Mention ORM objects), then dispatches `verify_url` over a `ThreadPoolExecutor` — keeps all ORM mutation on the main thread (`session.get(Mention, mention_id).tombstoned_at = now`). Periodic `session.commit()` every `commit_every` checks (per `feedback_long_llm_batch_commits` durability pattern). Skips mentions where `tombstoned_at IS NOT NULL`. Returns `BatchStats(checked, tombstoned, alive, by_reason)` where `by_reason` is a `dict[str, int]` keyed on the `note` strings.
- **TH4 — Aggregation filter inserted in `pulse_check/aggregation/a1.py`** at the existing `mentions` dict-comprehension (line ~305): added `Mention.tombstoned_at.is_(None)` AND-clause. Then immediately after the dict is built: `primary_tags = [t for t in primary_tags if t.mention_id in mentions]` (same for `secondary_tags`) — drops tags whose backing mention got filtered out. Then re-checks the existing `if not primary_tags and not secondary_tags` early-out so we don't emit zero-mention aggregate rows post-tombstone. Existing 90+ unit tests for `aggregate_a1` still pass — no fixture has `tombstoned_at` set.
- **TH5 — `pulse_check/api/schemas.py` MentionView extension** — added `tombstoned_at: datetime | None` + `tombstone_reason: str | None`. Updated the schema's module-header docstring to reflect that the session-13 deferral is now lifted (ownership-phrase deferral retained). `pulse_check/api/main.py` `_mention_to_view()` passes both fields through.
- **TH6 — Frontend type + chip wiring.** `frontend/src/lib/types.ts` `MentionView` matches the new shape. `frontend/src/components/atoms/VerbatimCard.tsx` `showTagRow2` predicate updated to also fire on `mention.tombstoned_at !== null`; rendered `<Chip tone="tombstone">link dead</Chip>` after verified / upvotes / rating in the tag-row-2 group. The `tombstone` chip tone existed in `Chip.tsx` already (session-13 forward-looking placement) with `bg-surface-alt text-fg-muted italic` styling.
- **TH7 — Fixture file update.** `frontend/src/fixtures/sample.ts` has 12 `MentionView` objects literal-constructed for visual smoke. Added `tombstoned_at: null, tombstone_reason: null` to each via a single multi-line `Edit replace_all=true` on the pattern `}],\n  },` — verified the literal substring appeared exactly 12 times before replacing.
- **TH8 — `scripts/verify_mention_links.py` CLI.** argparse template matched against `scripts/scrape.py`. Flags: `--limit N`, `--workers N` (default 16), `--dry-run`, `--commit-every N` (default 100). Opens a single `httpx.Client()` in a `with` block alongside `session_scope()`. DNS / refused exception scenarios use `raise httpx.ConnectError(...) from socket.gaierror(...)` / `raise httpx.ConnectError(...) from ConnectionRefusedError(...)` to drive the `__cause__` walk in `_classify_connect_error`. `verify_all_pending` tests reuse the project's existing `session` fixture from `tests/conftest.py`.

Catches future regressions where the filter is removed or the tag-drop step is skipped.

**Live findings (operator visibility).**
- **Reddit dominates the corpus more than I expected.** `SELECT source_type, COUNT(*) FROM mentions` returns reddit_comment 4288 + reddit_post 610 + youtube_chunk 119 + article 44 = 5061. So reddit is 4898 / 5061 = **96.8% of the entire mention corpus**. Any URL-health work that doesn't have a reddit story handles less than 4% of what we've gathered.
- **Reddit aggressively rate-limits anonymous concurrent HEAD requests.** Dry-run at workers=16 on the full corpus returned `by_reason={http_429: 4585 (90.6%), http_403: 33 (0.6%), ok: 443 (8.8%)}` in ~2 minutes wall-clock. Slowing to workers=4 on a limit=200 subsample lifted ok-rate to 70.5% — but that's still 30% noise on a slow path, and won't scale to the full corpus without breaking the quarterly-refresh wall-clock budget.
- **The 33 × http_403s are likely retailer bot-blocks.** Plain `httpx.HEAD` doesn't get past Cloudflare's / Akamai's bot-detection on amazon.com / bestbuy.com URLs. The catalog_discovery path uses `curl_cffi` Chrome 120 TLS impersonation for the same reason. Real link-rot detection on those URLs would need the same TLS-fingerprint path.
- **Conservative rule did real work even with bad signal.** Without the "leave alive on transient / ambiguous" stance, 4585 × http_429 + 33 × http_403 = 4618 mentions would have been false-positively tombstoned — that's 91.2% of the corpus, including basically everything reddit. The rule is what makes the verifier safe to ship BEFORE the throttling/auth path lands.
- **Session-13 lock-lift was clean and self-contained.** The `tombstone` chip tone was already defined in `Chip.tsx` (forward-looking placement from session 13); only the chip-tone-table comment in DESIGN_SYSTEM said "deferred." Lift was: (a) one VerbatimCard.tsx comment edit, (b) one JSX block addition, (c) one types.ts field addition, (d) one DESIGN_SYSTEM line edit. No other callers needed touching.
- **ARCH §9 doc drift was the load-bearing find of the session.** Original §9 (line 607) said tombstoned mentions "remain contributors to aggregates — the aggregate was computed from them; removing them retroactively would change published numbers." Implementation choice (filter in `aggregate_a1`) directly contradicts this. Per CLAUDE.md "When an implementation choice deviates from PRD / ARCHITECTURE / DESIGN_SYSTEM / TESTING / TASKS, Claude flags the deviation in plain English in-conversation, explains the reason, waits for operator approval" — the deviation was effectively flagged when I wrote the design proposal ("Aggregation filter scope: filter at aggregation only (proposed — affects future runs) vs also patching existing aggregates' mention_ids JSON; proposed: forward-only"), and operator approved with "go". But the docs reference itself wasn't flagged. Lesson for next deviation: check the relevant doc sections during design, not at wrap-up.
- **Doc-drift subagent hit a 529 Overloaded error.** Fell back to direct parallel reads + greps. Subagent reliability is non-zero risk on session-wrap; direct parallel reads completed in under a minute with no flakiness.

**Technical housekeeping (operator does not engage).**
- **TH1 — `compute_micro_f1_loose` greedy matching.** Algorithm: (a) exact `(aspect, polarity, intensity)` set-intersection matches consume their pair first; (b) remaining gold tuples iterate, each searching pred_remaining for an adjacent-intensity tuple within the same (aspect, polarity) group; (c) first hit consumes that pred and counts TP, else gold counts as FN; (d) any pred left over after step (c) counts as FP. Exact-first ordering prevents a bad case where (T,N,H) gold could be greedily consumed by (T,N,M) pred when (T,N,H) pred is also available. `_INTENSITY_INDEX = {LOW: 0, MEDIUM: 1, HIGH: 2}`. The `abs(diff) == 1` check excludes equal and excludes 2-bucket-apart.
- **TH2 — `EvalReport.passed` flips to `micro_loose.f1 >= THRESHOLD`.** Existing tests (`test_passed_true_when_f1_meets_threshold`, `test_passed_false_when_f1_below_threshold`) still pass because their fixtures give the same f1 under both metrics (perfect match = 1.0 on both; thermals-vs-battery = 0.0 on both since aspect differs). New `test_passed_gate_is_loose_not_strict` proves the gate-flip explicitly (strict 0.0, loose 1.0, passed=True).
- **TH3 — `report_to_dict` emits `micro_f1_loose` block alongside `micro_f1`.** Two test updates: `test_includes_all_top_level_keys` set bumped to include the new key; new `test_micro_f1_loose_serialized_alongside_strict` proves both render with their respective scores. Existing JSON consumers (none in the codebase) get the strict block unchanged.
- **TH4 — CLI summary format change.** Old: `Micro F1:     N.NNNN` + `Result:       PASS`. New: `Micro F1 (strict):  N.NNNN` + `Micro F1 (loose):   N.NNNN  <- gate` + `Result:             PASS`. Two `TestFormatSummary` tests updated; assertion strings rewritten to match the new column widths.
- **TH5 — Stage B integration test fixture pattern mirrors `tests/unit/aggregation/test_a1.py` style.** Helper `_seed_post_classifier_corpus()` inline-constructs Run / Product / Mention / MentionAttribution / AspectTag rows (no external fixture files). `BriefNarrative` stub built from `pulse_check.synthesis.contracts` pydantic models with one section + one claim citing real mention IDs from the seeded pool. `MagicMock(spec=AnthropicClient)` passed as `client` arg — never invoked because the stubs replace both LLM-coupled functions at the orchestrator's import site.

### 2026-05-19 — session 35: Wave 4 accessibility close + Wave 3 A2 path **parked** + ASUS / ASUS ROG merge (YAML + DB UPDATE) + `BriefExportButton` (single-page A4 PDF + HTML one-pager wired on Standalone) + `GlossaryDialog` (trimmed to 6 terms × 5 sections, on Standalone / Pair / Compare) + `EvidenceDrawer` verified-only filter hidden + home page CTAs converted to purple-outlined buttons + `backdrop-blur-md` + soft-lavender pill tint polish.

**Context entering.** Session 34 closed with the heatmap polish round shipped and three pre-bite forks queued (Wave 3 A2 · Sonnet aspect-labeler iteration · operator visual confirm). Operator opened session 35 by closing fork 3 ("I have checked visually, I can see all 59 products now") and asking for a recommendation between the remaining two. Recommendation was Wave 3 A2 with a thinness-first diagnostic; operator pushed back with a sharp framing — "Reddit is where people compare, not where they confirm" — leading to the parking decision. Session then pivoted into a long Wave-4-polish + UI-iteration arc driven by operator feedback against the live deployment (data quality, glossary affordance, ASUS grouping, brief export).

**Audit pass (session 34 → 35).** GREEN. 623/623 pytest · mypy 125 src clean · ruff clean · migration head `eb05da255474` · DB counts unchanged from session 34 (5061 mentions / 4,813 aspect_tags / 489 aggregates / 53 v2 briefs) · session-34 DESIGN_SYSTEM §6.1 / §6.3 / §6.4 patches verified · 11 Compare.tsx code claims verified · frontend bundle `index-B7gMvLmE.js` confirmed on disk · TASKS.md flat-checklist rule holding.

**Decisions (product-level) reached this session.**
- **D1 — Wave 3 A2 deliberation path PARKED.** Reddit corpus is structurally a comparison venue, not a confirmation venue: people compare options in threads, then leave to buy off-platform — so resolved-to-tracked-product deliberation is rare *by corpus property*, not by tagger failure (0/50 in the v2 gold-set sample per `project_reddit_deliberation_exploratory` memory). Proposed pivots in-session: (a) drop "who won" → reason-density landscape; (b) brand-pair instead of SKU-pair; (c) abort A2; (d) A1-derived pair brief synthesizing from `aggregates_aspect_sku` diffs with Sonnet. Operator landed on **drop the head-to-head brief workflow entirely for now** — feature-complete state without A2. A2 schema tables remain empty in the DB for future revival when corpus mix changes (BestBuy/Amazon ingestion would also help). ARCHITECTURE §3.2 annotated with the parking note.
- **D2 — Wave 4 polish closes the accessibility / empty-state / error-state gates.** Audit subagent flagged 6 areas; verification per `feedback_audit_subagent_verify` memory caught 2 false-positives (focus-rings and `prefers-reduced-motion` are already globally wired in `index.css`). Real gaps: Pair idle-state explainer card (replaces `null` return), `MultiSelectPopover` Escape-key close, `HeatCell` + sort-header `aria-label`s, About accordions `aria-controls`. All shipped. Loading skeletons explicitly deferred — text-based "Loading…" fallbacks already in place; visual skeleton component would be polish-on-polish.
- **D3 — `GlossaryDialog` trimmed from 15 entries to 6 entries × 5 sections.** Operator pushback: "that is a lot of items, which are implicitly understood?" Triage rule = a term must appear on screen as a label/jargon-y phrase AND a non-technical reader can't guess it from context. Drops: Polarity (never appears literally; chips say "positive"/"negative" directly) · Aspect (never as a literal word; column headers say "Thermals" etc.) · Verbatim (never as a label) · Citation (`CiteChip` shows IDs but never names the concept) · Total mentions (derives from Mention) · Run (kept tentatively then dropped). Five sections: Unit · How we score · How we count · Head-to-head · Output. Format = stacked definition-list (not table) because sub-bullets nest cleanly under the combined `Primary / Secondary / Long-tail attribution` entry; a table cell would cramp them.
- **D4 — `BriefExportButton` ships as a visual one-pager, NOT a text dump.** Operator-explicit on the spec: "doesn't need all the information but needs the important information while being within the one-page limit … make it a little bit visual/infographic instead of just all text death." Sheet = 4 stat tiles (mentions / pos aspects / neg aspects / avg net) + 11-aspect color-coded chip row + top 3 strengths + top 3 complaints (claim headers only, bracketed `[n]` cite numbers) + citations footer (`[n] → source · @author · date`). Six brainstorm decisions ratified in advance: truncate-with-link (no auto-shrink), bracketed numbers + footer (no inline snippets), browser-print PDF (no `html2pdf.js` dep), on-screen preview overlay (no direct download), Variant-A soft-tint button highlight, no config hot-reload mechanism. Component is generic over `BriefView` + `ProductDetail` so it transplants onto a future Pair brief unchanged.
- **D5 — ASUS / ASUS ROG merged into one brand.** Operator request: "group ASUS ROG products and ASUS products within one header ASUS." Done at the canonical config layer (YAML) AND at the DB layer. Distinct brand count: 7 → 6. Display names preserve the ROG line at the product level (e.g., "ROG Strix G16" still reads as ROG; only the company filter / brand banner unifies).
- **D6 — `EvidenceDrawer` verified-only filter HIDDEN.** `verified_share` is structurally 0% across all 489 aggregate rows because zero of the 5,061 ingested mentions carry a `verified_purchase` key in metadata. Reddit / YouTube / articles don't expose that concept — only BestBuy / Amazon retailer-review ingestion would. Until that lands (Wave 2 deferred plumbing), the filter is gated off in the UI. Aggregator + schema column retained.
- **D7 — Home page CTAs are purple-outlined buttons** that flip to solid purple on hover (`border-accent bg-surface text-accent hover:bg-accent hover:text-fg-on-accent`). Trailing `→` arrows dropped — the button block carries the affordance.
- **D8 — Soft-lavender pill highlight (Variant A) for the actions cluster.** `border-accent-soft bg-accent-soft text-accent hover:border-accent hover:text-accent-hover`. Operator initially reported the pill "not tinted at all" — root cause was 40%-opacity (`bg-accent-soft/40`) reading as near-white against the white surface. Bumped to full opacity + purple text.
- **D9 — `backdrop-blur-md` on both modal overlays** (Glossary and Export). Operator: "the export preview background is confusing." 12px Gaussian blur defocuses page content behind the modal without darkening it.

**Technical housekeeping (operator does not engage).**
- **TH1 — `/api/products` reads from the SQLite `products` table, not the YAML.** `products` is populated at ingest time from the YAML; the API hits the DB via `session.execute(select(Product))` on every request. Implication: changes to the YAML's `brand:` field don't propagate until either (a) re-ingest or (b) direct `UPDATE products SET brand=… WHERE …`. The ASUS merge initially looked like it didn't take effect because we patched the YAML and restarted uvicorn but forgot to update the DB. Resolved with a 1-line UPDATE statement; no uvicorn restart needed (SQLAlchemy hits the DB per-request).
- **TH2 — `BriefExportButton` uses `React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, BriefExportSheetProps>`.** Initial implementation attempted a custom `Object.assign` workaround to mimic forwardRef typing; broken at runtime because React strips `ref` from `props` before reaching the function component. Refactored to proper `forwardRef`. A4 sheet uses inline `React.CSSProperties` styles throughout (not Tailwind) so the `outerHTML` serialization for HTML download is self-contained.
- **TH3 — Print CSS strategy.** New `@media print` block in `frontend/src/index.css` activates when `BriefExportButton` toggles `body.printing` before `window.print()`. Rule set: `@page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 0 }`; `body.printing > *:not(.export-overlay)` hidden; overlay header (toolbar) hidden; overlay body padding/overflow neutralized; `.a4-sheet` shadow stripped. `pageBreakInside: "avoid"` + `maxHeight: 297mm` on the sheet so content never spills to page 2.
- **TH4 — Strengths / weaknesses extraction.** Walks `brief.narrative.sections` and routes by keyword on `heading` (`/strength|working|positive/` → strengths bucket; `/not working|weakness|complaint|negative/` → weaknesses bucket). Negative keywords checked first so a heading that contains both signals (e.g., "Low-signal strengths" vs "Low-signal weaknesses") routes correctly. First-half / second-half fallback if no headings match the keyword set. Top 3 claims of each bucket.
- **TH5 — Citation numbering.** `citedIds` collected as a `Set` over all 6 shown claims preserving insertion order; `citeNumber: Map<mention_id, number>` built once with sequential `[1]`, `[2]`, … indices so the bracketed body numbers align with the footer list. Lazy `api.mentions(citedIds)` fetch on overlay open, keyed by `mention_id` for footer rendering (source · author · date). Footer falls back gracefully (`source = "—"`) if the fetch fails — bracketed numbers still resolve.
- **TH6 — Aspect chip color buckets.** Three tones: `pos` (net > +0.1, `success-soft` fill), `neg` (net < -0.1, `danger-soft` fill), `neutral` (otherwise, `warning-soft` fill). Fourth bucket `none` (gray fill) for aspects with `total_mentions === 0`.
- **TH7 — No backend / schema / LLM changes this session.** $0 LLM spend; cumulative across sessions remains ~$19.70.

**Live findings (operator visibility).**
- **The ASUS merge after YAML edit + uvicorn restart still showed split brand in the live UI.** Took two iterations: first attempted YAML-only (no effect because of TH1), then YAML + uvicorn restart (still no effect because the running DB had stale ingest data), then YAML + DB UPDATE (fixed). The `products` table is the source of truth for runtime brand display — this was non-obvious and worth keeping in mind for future config-level changes that should propagate without a re-scrape.
- **Audit subagent two false-positives** caught and rejected per `feedback_audit_subagent_verify` memory: (a) focus-rings already global via `*:focus-visible` in `index.css`; (b) `prefers-reduced-motion` already wired in the same file. Subagent was grepping for per-element Tailwind classes and missed the global rules. Verified by direct read of `index.css` before applying any per-element focus fixes.
- **`verified_share` = 0.00 across all 489 aggregate rows** confirmed via direct SQL: `SELECT MIN(verified_share), MAX(verified_share), AVG(verified_share) FROM aggregates_aspect_sku WHERE run_id='run_wave5_v1'` returned `(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 489)`. Cross-checked against `mentions.metadata` — `json_extract(metadata, '$.verified_purchase') IS NOT NULL` count = **0** across all 5,061 mentions.

**Context entering.** Session 33 closed with 6 frontend UI polish edits + the README runbook rewrite shipped, and 4 pre-bite forks queued: Wave 3 A2 path · Sonnet aspect-labeler iteration · Heatmap UI further polish · operator visual confirm. Operator opened session 34 with the explicit pick of the heatmap polish fork ("go"). Polish landed cleanly; mid-session operator pulled in two adjacent changes — make `Company` sortable too, and move the legend from below the grid to above. Then a screenshot surfaced a silent filter bug ("46 of 59 products" with all filters set to "all") which turned out to be the load-bearing find of the session.

**Audit pass (session 33 → 34).** GREEN. 623/623 pytest · mypy 125 src clean · ruff clean · migration head `eb05da255474` · DB counts unchanged (5061 mentions / 4,813 aspect_tags / 489 aggregates / 53 v2 briefs) · ARCH §6.3 header field present · bundle `index-7Qcz5hSE.js` shipped. Drift flagged at session-33 close: §6.1 About + §6.3 Compare carried session-13 stubs not yet reconciled with the shipped reality — patched this session.

**Decisions (product-level) reached this session.**
- **D1 — Sortable headers cycle asc → desc → clear.** First click ascending (worst-sentiment / A-by-brand on top), second click descending, third click clears back to the brand-grouped Alienware-pinned default. Discriminated `SortKey` union (`{kind:"aspect",aspect}` | `{kind:"company"}`). Operator pulled in `Company` sortability mid-session; `Product` column intentionally kept non-sortable since brand grouping already carries its ordering.
- **D2 — Cascading filters, top-down.** `Company` → `Size` → `Product`. Operator-locked: "don't make the user make selections for all 3 in a disconnected way." Implementation: downstream popover items derived from upstream selections; downstream selections auto-rebase to "all available" on upstream change. Trade-off accepted: user-pinned narrowings in `Size` / `Product` are wiped when upstream changes.
- **D3 — Color-scale legend moves above the grid.** Between `FilterPanel` and the grid wrapper. Operator-explicit. Replaced the prose footer with 3 sentiment swatches + Alienware-edge marker + inline action hints; copy now mentions "click any column header to sort" (was "aspect column").
- **D4 — Size-less products always pass the size filter.** "Lenovo Legion 7 (AMD, non-Pro)", "Legion 7i", "Legion 9i" have no inch-suffix in their `display_name`. They're real products; the size filter is informational, not gating. `filteredProducts` size check inverted: `if (s !== null && !sizes.has(s)) return false;` (was `s === null || !sizes.has(s)`).
- **D5 — 11-aspect heatmap view is complete.** Operator asked: "is there any other field we are missing outside of the 11?" Subagent verified the canonical `pulse_check.storage.enums.Aspect` enum has exactly 11 values; `/api/compare` returns all of them; no taxonomy fields filtered upstream. Richer per-cell signals (intensity_counts, verified_share, by_source, by_recency) exist on `aggregates_aspect_sku` but are intentionally drill-down-only via `EvidenceDrawer` + Standalone — not surfaced on the cross-product grid (operator pilot discipline holds: no rare-event composites on the 59×11 view).

**Technical housekeeping (operator does not engage).**
- **TH1 — Row-hover affordance via `display:contents` group.** `ProductRow` children wrapped in `<div className="contents group">` so the wrapper is layout-transparent to the parent grid but still receives `:hover`. `HeatCell` and the sticky Company / Product cells take `group-hover:brightness-[0.97]`; the Product cell additionally underlines its name on row hover. Single-element strategy; no JS hover-tracking state needed.
- **TH2 — Screen-size regex loosened from `\b(13|14|15|16|17|18)\b` to `(?<!\d)(13|14|15|16|17|18)(?!\d)`.** Word-boundary required non-word chars on both sides; "16x", "16s", "Z13", "X16" all failed. Lookbehind/lookahead "not a digit" still rejects "160", "1314" false positives. Verified live against the 59-product corpus: 1×13" / 6×14" / 7×15" / 23×16" / 9×17" / 10×18" = 56 sized + 3 size-null = 59 total.
- **TH3 — Cascade implementation.** Two new `useEffect`s in `Compare.tsx`: one watches `selectedCompanies` and writes `selectedSizes`; another watches `selectedCompanies` + `selectedSizes` and writes `selectedProducts`. Both gated on `state.kind === "ready"` + upstream `!== null` so they don't fire pre-seed. `sizeItems` / `productItems` memos also rebuilt as cascade-aware so the popover lists narrow visually in sync with the auto-rebase. `allSizes` memo dropped (subsumed by `sizeItems`).
- **TH4 — Aspect-sort sentinel.** Cells with no mentions or no data sort to ±Infinity based on direction so they always cluster at the bottom — "no data → last" regardless of asc/desc. Within-tie order preserved.
- **TH5 — Company-sort tiebreaker.** `Array.prototype.sort` is stable in V8 / SpiderMonkey; equal-brand rows keep `filteredProducts` order, which itself preserves the backend's Alienware-pinned-then-original ordering.
- **TH6 — No backend / schema / LLM changes this session.** 0 LLM spend; session 34 cumulative spend across sessions remains ~$19.70.

**Live findings (operator visibility).**
- **"Showing 46 of 59 products" was a silent filter bug, not a backend / data issue.** The screen-size regex pre-existed from session 32's heatmap ship; the bug was latent until a `16x` model landed in the corpus. The "46 of 59" line read like a UX hint but was actually exposing 13 invisible exclusions. Operator screenshot showed Alienware with 3 products visible; the missing 4th (`Alienware 16x Aurora`) was the most legible symptom.
- **The 13 silently-filtered products** spanned 6 brands: Alienware (`16x Aurora`), ROG (`Flow Z13`, `Flow X16`, possibly others on second look), Lenovo (`Legion 7 (AMD, non-Pro)`, `Legion 7i`, `Legion 9i` — these are the 3 size-null cases), Acer (`Predator Neo 16s`). Recovery is uniform across the corpus once the regex + null-handling are fixed.
- **Live API verification post-fix.** Hit `http://localhost:8765/api/compare`, bucketed all 59 products with the new regex: 1×13" / 6×14" / 7×15" / 23×16" / 9×17" / 10×18" + 3 size-null. Total = 59. Alienware list = `[16 Area-51, 16 Aurora, 16x Aurora, 18 Area-51]`. Confirmed before declaring done.

Patch notes "session 34" annotated inline.

- **D4 — `chosen_product_id` XOR `chosen_external_name`.** Mutually exclusive. Tracked winner → `chosen_product_id` set, external null. External winner → `chosen_external_name` set, `chosen_product_id` null. Unresolved → both null. Parser enforces: if both arrive from the model, tracked wins (more specific); external dropped with warning.
- **D5 — `is_resolved` ⇔ at least one winner channel set.** Either resolution path counts. Lets us count total decisive threads (regardless of internal/external winner), filter to tracked-only when needed, and aggregate external-winner losses separately. Reason-labeling gate at `deliberation_gold_set.py:420` remains `chosen_product_id is None` (external-winner threads have `chosen_product_id` null → still correctly skipped from reason labeling).
- **D6 — Stage the 28.e config flips.** Stage 1 = `discovered_urls_sources` flipped alone; Reddit off; RSS at 3mo. Stage 2 = reverse Reddit + RSS flips. Isolates new-code-path failures from bandwidth-heavy paths. Operator-approved per session-26 caution memory.
